Mathlib.GroupTheory.Abelianization.Defs

AbelianizationdefMathlib.GroupTheory.Abelianization.Defs, type
Wording
abelianization of G (from the name)
English
Let $G$ be a set. Then an abelianization of $G$ is an element of the function that maps $G$ to the set of quotients of $G$ and the commutator subgroup of $G$.
Formula
$G \in \mathrm{Set} \vdash \mathrm{Abelianization} (G):= \lambda G . \operatorname{Quotient} (G , \mathrm{commutator} (G))$
French
Soit $G$ un ensemble. Alors une abélianisation de $G$ est un élément de la fonction qui envoie $G$ à l'ensemble de quotients de $G$ et le sous-groupe de commutateurs de $G$.
Abelianization.equivOfCommdefMathlib.GroupTheory.Abelianization.Defs, function or constant
Wording
the equivalence (from the name)
English
The equivalence is a multiplicative equivalence of $H$ and the set of abelianizations of $H$.
Formula
$\operatorname{equivOfComm} \in \mathrm{MulEquiv} (H , \mathrm{Abelianization} (H))$
French
L'équivalence est une équivalence multiplicative de $H$ et l'ensemble d'abélianisations de $H$.
Abelianization.liftdefMathlib.GroupTheory.Abelianization.Defs, function or constant
Wording
the lift (from the name)
English
The lift is an equivalence from the set of monoid homomorphisms of $G$ and $A$ to the set of monoid homomorphisms of the set of abelianizations of $G$ and $A$.
Formula
$\operatorname{lift} \in \EquivMACRoAL {\mathrm{MonoidHom} (G , A)}{\mathrm{MonoidHom} (\mathrm{Abelianization} (G), A)}$
French
Le relèvement est une équivalence de l'ensemble d'homomorphismes de monoïdes de $G$ et $A$ à l'ensemble d'homomorphismes de monoïdes de l'ensemble d'abélianisations de $G$ et $A$.
Abelianization.mapdefMathlib.GroupTheory.Abelianization.Defs, function or constant
Wording
the map of f (from the name)
English
Let $f$ be a monoid homomorphism of $G$ and $H$. Then the map of $f$ is a monoid homomorphism of the set of abelianizations of $G$ and the set of abelianizations of $H$ defined as $\operatorname{lift}$ applied to the composition of $\operatorname{of}$ and $f$.
Formula
$f \in \mathrm{MonoidHom} (G , H)\vdash \operatorname{map} (f):= \operatorname{lift} (\operatorname{comp} (\operatorname{of} , f))\in \mathrm{MonoidHom} (\mathrm{Abelianization} (G), \mathrm{Abelianization} (H))$
French
Soit $f$ un homomorphisme de monoïdes de $G$ et $H$. Alors l'application de $f$ est un homomorphisme de monoïdes de l'ensemble d'abélianisations de $G$ et l'ensemble d'abélianisations de $H$ défini comme $\operatorname{lift}$ appliqué à la composition de $\operatorname{of}$ et $f$.
Abelianization.ofdefMathlib.GroupTheory.Abelianization.Defs, function or constant
Wording
no wording
English
$\operatorname{of}$ is a monoid homomorphism of $G$ and the set of abelianizations of $G$.
Formula
$\operatorname{of} \in \mathrm{MonoidHom} (G , \mathrm{Abelianization} (G))$
French
$\operatorname{of}$ est un homomorphisme de monoïdes de $G$ et l'ensemble d'abélianisations de $G$.
MulEquiv.abelianizationCongrdefMathlib.GroupTheory.Abelianization.Defs, function or constant
Wording
the abelianization congruence of e (from the name)
English
Let $e$ be a multiplicative equivalence of $G$ and $H$. Then the abelianization congruence of $e$ is a multiplicative equivalence of the set of abelianizations of $G$ and the set of abelianizations of $H$.
Formula
$e \in \mathrm{MulEquiv} (G , H)\vdash \operatorname{abelianizationCongr} (e)\in \mathrm{MulEquiv} (\mathrm{Abelianization} (G), \mathrm{Abelianization} (H))$
French
Soit $e$ une équivalence multiplicative de $G$ et $H$. Alors la congruence d'abélianisations de $e$ est une équivalence multiplicative de l'ensemble d'abélianisations de $G$ et l'ensemble d'abélianisations de $H$.

Mathlib.GroupTheory.ArchimedeanDensely

LinearOrderedAddCommGroup.int_orderAddMonoidIso_of_isLeast_posdefMathlib.GroupTheory.ArchimedeanDensely, function or constant
Wording
the integer order additive monoid isomorphism of h2 (from the name)
English
Assume that $\mathrm{IsLeast}$ holds for $\operatorname{ofPred}$ applied to the function that maps $y$ to $\mathrm{Lt} (0 , y)$ and $x_{2}$. Then the integer order additive monoid isomorphism of $h_{2}$ is an order additive monoid isomorphism of $G$ and $\Integer$.
Formula
$\mathrm{IsLeast} (\operatorname{ofPred} (\lambda y . \mathrm{Lt} (0 , y)), x_{2})\vdash \mathrm{LinearOrderedAddCommGroup\_\_int\_orderAddMonoidIso\_of\_isLeast\_pos} (h_{2})\in \mathrm{OrderAddMonoidIso} (G , \Integer)$
French
Supposons que $\mathrm{IsLeast}$ tient pour $\operatorname{ofPred}$ appliqué à la fonction qui envoie $y$ à $\mathrm{Lt} (0 , y)$ et $x_{2}$. Alors l'isomorphisme de monoïdes additif d'ordres entier de $h_{2}$ est un isomorphisme de monoïdes additif d'ordres de $G$ et $\Integer$.
LinearOrderedAddCommGroup.zmultiplesOrderAddMonoidIsodefMathlib.GroupTheory.ArchimedeanDensely, function or constant
Wording
no wording
English
Let $x_{4}$ be an element of $G$. Let $y$ be an element of $\mathrm{G'}$ such that $\EqtMACRoH {x_{4}}{0}$, if and only if $\EqtMACRoH {y}{0}$. Then $\operatorname{zmultiplesOrderAddMonoidIso}$ applied to $x_{4}$, $y$ and $\mathrm{hxy}$ is an order additive monoid isomorphism of $\SubtypeSet {x_{5}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoT {\operatorname{zmultiples} (x_{4})}{x_{5}})}$ and $\SubtypeSet {x_{6}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoT {\operatorname{zmultiples} (y)}{x_{6}})}$.
Formula
$x_{4} \in G , y \in \mathrm{G'} , \EqtMACRoJ {x_{4}}{0}\leftrightarrow \EqtMACRoJ {y}{0}\vdash \operatorname{zmultiplesOrderAddMonoidIso} (x_{4} , y , \mathrm{hxy})\in \mathrm{OrderAddMonoidIso} (\SubtypeSet {x_{5}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoAA {\operatorname{zmultiples} (x_{4})}{x_{5}})}, \SubtypeSet {x_{6}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoAA {\operatorname{zmultiples} (y)}{x_{6}})})$
French
Soit $x_{4}$ un élément de $G$. Soit $y$ un élément de $\mathrm{G'}$ tel que $\EqtMACRoH {x_{4}}{0}$ si et seulement si $y$ est égal à $0$. Alors $\operatorname{zmultiplesOrderAddMonoidIso}$ appliqué à $x_{4}$, $y$ et $\mathrm{hxy}$ est un isomorphisme de monoïdes additif d'ordres de $\SubtypeSet {x_{5}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoT {\operatorname{zmultiples} (x_{4})}{x_{5}})}$ et $\SubtypeSet {x_{6}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoT {\operatorname{zmultiples} (y)}{x_{6}})}$.
LinearOrderedCommGroup.multiplicative_int_orderMonoidIso_of_isLeast_one_ltdefMathlib.GroupTheory.ArchimedeanDensely, function or constant
Wording
the multiplicative integer order monoid isomorphism of h2 (from the name)
English
Assume that $\mathrm{IsLeast}$ holds for $\operatorname{ofPred}$ applied to the function that maps $y$ to $\mathrm{Lt} (1 , y)$ and $x_{2}$. Then the multiplicative integer order monoid isomorphism of $h_{2}$ is an order monoid isomorphism of $G$ and the set of multiplicatives of $\Integer$ defined as $\operatorname{toMultiplicativeRight}$ applied to the integer order additive monoid isomorphism of $h_{2}$.
Formula
$\mathrm{IsLeast} (\operatorname{ofPred} (\lambda y . \mathrm{Lt} (1 , y)), x_{2})\vdash \mathrm{LinearOrderedCommGroup\_\_multiplicative\_int\_orderMonoidIso\_of\_isLeast\_one\_lt} (h_{2}):= \operatorname{toMultiplicativeRight} (\mathrm{LinearOrderedAddCommGroup\_\_int\_orderAddMonoidIso\_of\_isLeast\_pos} (h_{2}))\in \mathrm{OrderMonoidIso} (G , \mathrm{Multiplicative} (\Integer))$
French
Supposons que $\mathrm{IsLeast}$ tient pour $\operatorname{ofPred}$ appliqué à la fonction qui envoie $y$ à $\mathrm{Lt} (1 , y)$ et $x_{2}$. Alors l'isomorphisme de monoïdes d'ordres entier multiplicatif de $h_{2}$ est un isomorphisme de monoïdes d'ordres de $G$ et l'ensemble de multiplicatifs de $\Integer$ défini comme $\operatorname{toMultiplicativeRight}$ appliqué à l'isomorphisme de monoïdes additif d'ordres entier de $h_{2}$.

Mathlib.GroupTheory.Commensurable

AddSubgroup.CommensurabledefMathlib.GroupTheory.Commensurable, predicate
Wording
no wording
English
Let $H$ and $K$ be additive subgroups of $G$. Then $\operatorname{Commensurable}$ holds for $H$ and $K$, if $\operatorname{IsFiniteRelIndex}$ holds for $H$ and $K$ and $\operatorname{IsFiniteRelIndex}$ holds for $K$ and $H$.
Formula
$H , K \in \mathrm{AddSubgroup} (G)\vdash \operatorname{Commensurable} (H , K):\Leftrightarrow \operatorname{IsFiniteRelIndex} (H , K)\wedge \operatorname{IsFiniteRelIndex} (K , H)$
French
Soient $H$ et $K$ des sous-groupes additifs de $G$. Alors $\operatorname{Commensurable}$ tient pour $H$ et $K$ si $\operatorname{IsFiniteRelIndex}$ tient pour $H$ et $K$ et $\operatorname{IsFiniteRelIndex}$ tient pour $K$ et $H$.
Subgroup.CommensurabledefMathlib.GroupTheory.Commensurable, predicate
Wording
no wording
English
Let $H$ and $K$ be subgroups of $G$. Then $\operatorname{Commensurable}$ holds for $H$ and $K$, if $\operatorname{IsFiniteRelIndex}$ holds for $H$ and $K$ and $\operatorname{IsFiniteRelIndex}$ holds for $K$ and $H$.
Formula
$H , K \in \mathrm{Subgroup} (G)\vdash \operatorname{Commensurable} (H , K):\Leftrightarrow \operatorname{IsFiniteRelIndex} (H , K)\wedge \operatorname{IsFiniteRelIndex} (K , H)$
French
Soient $H$ et $K$ des sous-groupes de $G$. Alors $\operatorname{Commensurable}$ tient pour $H$ et $K$ si $\operatorname{IsFiniteRelIndex}$ tient pour $H$ et $K$ et $\operatorname{IsFiniteRelIndex}$ tient pour $K$ et $H$.
Subgroup.Commensurable.commensuratordefMathlib.GroupTheory.Commensurable, function or constant
Wording
no wording
English
Let $H$ be a subgroup of $G$. Then $\operatorname{commensurator}$ applied to $H$ is a subgroup of $G$.
Formula
$H \in \mathrm{Subgroup} (G)\vdash \operatorname{commensurator} (H)\in \mathrm{Subgroup} (G)$
French
Soit $H$ un sous-groupe de $G$. Alors $\operatorname{commensurator}$ appliqué à $H$ est un sous-groupe de $G$.

Mathlib.GroupTheory.Commutator.Basic

addCommutatordefMathlib.GroupTheory.Commutator.Basic, function or constant
Wording
the commutator subgroup of G (from the docstring)
English
Let $G$ be a set. Then the commutator subgroup of $G$ is an additive subgroup of $G$ defined as the bracket of the top and the top.
Formula
$G \in \mathrm{Set} \vdash \mathrm{addCommutator} (G):= \operatorname{bracket} (\operatorname{top} , \operatorname{top})\in \mathrm{AddSubgroup} (G)$
French
Soit $G$ un ensemble. Alors le sous-groupe de commutateurs de $G$ est un sous-groupe additif de $G$ défini comme le crochet du plus grand élément et le plus grand élément.
addCommutatorRepresentativesdefMathlib.GroupTheory.Commutator.Basic, function or constant
Wording
the additive commutator representative of G (from the name)
English
Let $G$ be a set. Then the additive commutator representative of $G$ is an element of $\wp \mathrm{Prod} (G , G)$.
Formula
$G \in \mathrm{Set} \vdash \mathrm{addCommutatorRepresentatives} (G)\in \wp \mathrm{Prod} (G , G)$
French
Soit $G$ un ensemble. Alors le représentant de commutateurs additif de $G$ est un élément de $\wp \mathrm{Prod} (G , G)$.
addCommutatorSetdefMathlib.GroupTheory.Commutator.Basic, function or constant
Wording
the additive commutator set of G (from the name)
English
Let $G$ be a set. Then the additive commutator set of $G$ is an element of $\wp G$ defined as $\operatorname{ofPred}$ applied to the function that maps $g$ to the proposition that there exist elements $x_{1}$ and $x_{2}$ of $G$, such that the bracket of $x_{1}$ and $x_{2}$ is equal to $g$.
Formula
$G \in \mathrm{Set} \vdash \mathrm{addCommutatorSet} (G):= \operatorname{ofPred} (\lambda g . \exists x_{1} \in G . \exists x_{2} \in G . \EqtMACRoJ {\operatorname{bracket} (x_{1} , x_{2})}{g})\in \wp G$
French
Soit $G$ un ensemble. Alors l'ensemble de commutateurs additif de $G$ est un élément de $\wp G$ défini comme $\operatorname{ofPred}$ appliqué à la fonction qui envoie $g$ à la proposition qu'il existent des éléments $x_{1}$ et $x_{2}$ de $G$ tel que le crochet de $x_{1}$ et $x_{2}$ soit égal à $g$.
closureAddCommutatorRepresentativesdefMathlib.GroupTheory.Commutator.Basic, function or constant
Wording
the closure additive commutator representative of G (from the name)
English
Let $G$ be a set. Then the closure additive commutator representative of $G$ is an additive subgroup of $G$ defined as the closure of the union of the image of $\ProdfstMACRoZ$ and the additive commutator representative of $G$ and the image of $\ProdsndMACRoAA$ and the additive commutator representative of $G$.
Formula
$G \in \mathrm{Set} \vdash \mathrm{closureAddCommutatorRepresentatives} (G):= \operatorname{closure} (\UnionunionMACRoAI {\operatorname{image} (\ProdfstMACRoAG , \mathrm{addCommutatorRepresentatives} (G))}{\operatorname{image} (\ProdsndMACRoAH , \mathrm{addCommutatorRepresentatives} (G))})\in \mathrm{AddSubgroup} (G)$
French
Soit $G$ un ensemble. Alors le représentant de commutateurs additif de clôtures de $G$ est un sous-groupe additif de $G$ défini comme la clôture de l'union de l'image de $\ProdfstMACRoZ$ et le représentant de commutateurs additif de $G$ et l'image de $\ProdsndMACRoAA$ et le représentant de commutateurs additif de $G$.

commProbdefMathlib.GroupTheory.CommutingProbability, function or constant
Wording
the commutative probability of M (from the name, with words of the lexicon)
English
Let $M$ be a set. Then the commutative probability of $M$ is a rational number defined as the quotient of the cardinality of $\SubtypeSet {p}{\mathrm{Commute} (\ProdfstMACRoZ {p}, \ProdsndMACRoAA {p})}$ and the exponentiation of the cardinality of $M$ to $2$.
Formula
$M \in \mathrm{Set} \vdash \mathrm{commProb} (M):= \frac{ \operatorname{card} (\SubtypeSet {p}{\mathrm{Commute} (\ProdfstMACRoAG {p}, \ProdsndMACRoAH {p})})}{\operatorname{card} (M)^ {2}}\in \Rational$
French
Soit $M$ un ensemble. Alors la probabilité commutative de $M$ est un nombre rationnel défini comme le quotient entre la cardinalité de $\SubtypeSet {p}{\mathrm{Commute} (\ProdfstMACRoZ {p}, \ProdsndMACRoAA {p})}$ et la puissance de la cardinalité de $M$ en $2$.

Mathlib.GroupTheory.Complement

AddSubgroup.IsComplementdefMathlib.GroupTheory.Complement, predicate
Wording
no wording
English
Let $S$ and $T$ be elements of $\wp G$. Then $\operatorname{IsComplement}$ holds for $S$ and $T$, if the function that maps $x_{1}$ to $\ProdfstMACRoZ {x_{1}}+ \ProdsndMACRoAA {x_{1}}$ is bijective.
Formula
$S , T \in \wp G \vdash \operatorname{IsComplement} (S , T):\Leftrightarrow \operatorname{Bijective} (\lambda x_{1} . \ProdfstMACRoAG {x_{1}}+ \ProdsndMACRoAH {x_{1}})$
French
Soient $S$ et $T$ des éléments de $\wp G$. Alors $\operatorname{IsComplement}$ tient pour $S$ et $T$ si la fonction qui envoie $x_{1}$ à $\ProdfstMACRoZ {x_{1}}+ \ProdsndMACRoAA {x_{1}}$ est bijective.
AddSubgroup.IsComplement'defMathlib.GroupTheory.Complement, predicate
Wording
no wording
English
Let $H$ and $K$ be additive subgroups of $G$. Then $\operatorname{IsComplement'}$ holds for $H$ and $K$, if $\operatorname{IsComplement}$ holds for $H$ and $K$.
Formula
$H , K \in \mathrm{AddSubgroup} (G)\vdash \operatorname{IsComplement'} (H , K):\Leftrightarrow \operatorname{IsComplement} (H , K)$
French
Soient $H$ et $K$ des sous-groupes additifs de $G$. Alors $\operatorname{IsComplement'}$ tient pour $H$ et $K$ si $\operatorname{IsComplement}$ tient pour $H$ et $K$.
AddSubgroup.IsComplement.leftQuotientEquivdefMathlib.GroupTheory.Complement, function or constant
Wording
the left quotient equivalence of hS (from the name)
English
Assume that $\operatorname{IsComplement}$ holds for $S$ and $H$. Then the left quotient equivalence of $\mathrm{hS}$ is an equivalence from the set of quotients of $G$ and $H$ to the set of elements of $S$.
Formula
$\operatorname{IsComplement} (S , H)\vdash \operatorname{leftQuotientEquiv} (\mathrm{hS})\in \EquivMACRoAL {\operatorname{Quotient} (G , H)}{\operatorname{Elem} (S)}$
French

Wording
the right quotient equivalence of hT (from the name)
English

Formula
$\operatorname{IsComplement} (H , T)\vdash \operatorname{rightQuotientEquiv} (\mathrm{hT})\in \EquivMACRoAL {\mathrm{Quotient} (\operatorname{rightRel} (H))}{\operatorname{Elem} (T)}$

AddSubgroup.IsComplement.toLeftFundefMathlib.GroupTheory.Complement, function or constant
Wording
the left function of hS and x1 (from the name)
English
Assume that $\operatorname{IsComplement}$ holds for $S$ and $H$. Let $x_{1}$ be an element of $G$. Then the left function of $\mathrm{hS}$ and $x_{1}$ is an element of $S$ defined as $\operatorname{comp}$ applied to $\operatorname{coe}$ applied to the left quotient equivalence of $\mathrm{hS}$, $\operatorname{mk''}$ and $x_{1}$.
Formula
$\operatorname{IsComplement} (S , H), x_{1} \in G \vdash \operatorname{toLeftFun} (\mathrm{hS} , x_{1}):= \operatorname{comp} (\operatorname{coe} (\operatorname{leftQuotientEquiv} (\mathrm{hS})), \operatorname{mk''} , x_{1})\in \operatorname{Elem} (S)$
French
Supposons que $\operatorname{IsComplement}$ tient pour $S$ et $H$. Soit $x_{1}$ un élément de $G$. Alors la fonction gauche de $\mathrm{hS}$ et $x_{1}$ est un élément de $S$ défini comme $\operatorname{comp}$ appliqué à $\operatorname{coe}$ appliqué à l'équivalence de quotients gauche de $\mathrm{hS}$, $\operatorname{mk''}$ et $x_{1}$.
AddSubgroup.IsComplement.toRightFundefMathlib.GroupTheory.Complement, function or constant
Wording
the right function of hT and x1 (from the name)
English
Assume that $\operatorname{IsComplement}$ holds for $H$ and $T$. Let $x_{1}$ be an element of $G$. Let $c$ be a congruence relation of $M$. Let $f$ be a monoid homomorphism of $N$ and $M$ such that $\operatorname{Surjective}$ holds for $\operatorname{coe}$ applied to $f$. Then the preimage quotient equivalence of $c$ and $f$ is a multiplicative equivalence of the set of quotients of the preimage of $\operatorname{coe}$ applied to $f$ and $c$ and the set of quotients of $c$.
Formula
$c \in \mathrm{Con} (M), f \in \mathrm{MonoidHom} (N , M), \operatorname{Surjective} (\operatorname{coe} (f))\vdash \operatorname{comapQuotientEquivOfSurj} (c , f , \mathrm{hf})\in \mathrm{MulEquiv} (\operatorname{Quotient} (\operatorname{comap} (\operatorname{coe} (f), \mathrm{MonoidHom\_\_map\_mul} (f), c)), \operatorname{Quotient} (c))$
French
Soit $c$ une relation de congruences de $M$. Soit $f$ un homomorphisme de monoïdes de $N$ et $M$ tel que $\operatorname{Surjective}$ tienne pour $\operatorname{coe}$ appliqué à $f$. Alors l'équivalence de quotients de préimages de $c$ et $f$ est une équivalence multiplicative de l'ensemble de quotients de la préimage de $\operatorname{coe}$ appliqué à $f$ et $c$ et l'ensemble de quotients de $c$.
Con.congrdefMathlib.GroupTheory.Congruence.Basic, function or constant
Wording
the congruence of e and h (from the name)
English
Let $e$ be a multiplicative equivalence of $M$ and $N$ such that $c$ is equal to the preimage of $\operatorname{coe}$ applied to $e$ and $d$. Then the congruence of $e$ and $h$ is a multiplicative equivalence of the set of quotients of $c$ and the set of quotients of $d$.
Formula
$e \in \mathrm{MulEquiv} (M , N), \EqtMACRoJ {c}{\operatorname{comap} (\operatorname{coe} (e), \mathrm{map\_mul} (e), d)}\vdash \operatorname{congr} (e , h)\in \mathrm{MulEquiv} (\operatorname{Quotient} (c), \operatorname{Quotient} (d))$
French
Soit $e$ une équivalence multiplicative de $M$ et $N$ telle que $c$ soit égal à la préimage de $\operatorname{coe}$ appliqué à $e$ et $d$. Alors la congruence de $e$ et $h$ est une équivalence multiplicative de l'ensemble de quotients de $c$ et l'ensemble de quotients de $d$.
Con.ofSubmonoiddefMathlib.GroupTheory.Congruence.Basic, function or constant
Wording
the congruence relation of N and H (from the docstring)
English
Let $N$ be a submonoid of $\mathrm{Prod}$ applied to $M$ and $M$ such that the function that maps $x_{1}$ and $y$ to $\MembershipmemMACRoT {N}{(\ProdmkMACRoU {x_{1}}{y})}$ is an equivalence relation. Then the congruence relation of $N$ and $H$ is a congruence relation of $M$.
Formula
$N \in \mathrm{Submonoid} (\mathrm{Prod} (M , M)), \mathrm{Equivalence} (\lambda x_{1} , y . \MembershipmemMACRoAA {N}{(\ProdmkMACRoAB {x_{1}}{y})})\vdash \operatorname{ofSubmonoid} (N , H)\in \mathrm{Con} (M)$
French

Con.pidefMathlib.GroupTheory.Congruence.Basic, function or constant
Wording
the product of C (from the docstring)
English
Let $C$ be a function from elements $i_{1}$ of $x$ to congruence relations of $f$ applied to $i_{1}$. Then the product of $i_{1}$ is a congruence relation of the set of functions from elements $i$ of $x$ to elements of $f$ applied to $i$.
Formula

Soit $M$ un ensemble. Alors les relations de congruences de $M$ sont un type de base.
Con.QuotientdefMathlib.GroupTheory.Congruence.Defs, type
Wording
quotient of c (from the name)
English
Let $c$ be a congruence relation of $M$. Then a quotient of $c$ is an element of the function that maps $c$ to $\mathrm{Quotient}$ applied to $\operatorname{toSetoid}$ applied to $c$.
Formula
$c \in \mathrm{Con} (M)\vdash \operatorname{Quotient} (c):= \lambda c . \mathrm{Quotient} (\operatorname{toSetoid} (c))$
French
Soit $c$ une relation de congruences de $M$. Alors un quotient de $c$ est un élément de la fonction qui envoie $c$ à $\mathrm{Quotient}$ appliqué à $\operatorname{toSetoid}$ appliqué à $c$.

Wording
the preimage of f and c (from the name)
English

AddCon.liftdefMathlib.GroupTheory.Congruence.Hom, function or constant
Wording
the lift of c and f (from the name)
English
Let $c$ be an additive congruence relation of $M$. Let $f$ be an additive monoid homomorphism of $M$ and $P$ such that $c$ is less than or equal to the kernel of $f$. Then the lift of $c$ and $f$ is an additive monoid homomorphism of the set of quotients of $c$ and $P$.
Formula
$c \in \mathrm{AddCon} (M), f \in \mathrm{AddMonoidHom} (M , P), c \leq \operatorname{ker} (f)\vdash \operatorname{lift} (c , f , H)\in \mathrm{AddMonoidHom} (\operatorname{Quotient} (c), P)$
French

Soit $c$ une relation de congruences de $M$. Soit $f$ un homomorphisme de monoïdes de $M$ et $P$ tel que $c$ soit inférieur ou égal au noyau de $f$. Alors le relèvement de $c$ et $f$ est un homomorphisme de monoïdes de l'ensemble de quotients de $c$ et $P$.

AddMonoid.coprodCondefMathlib.GroupTheory.Coprod.Basic, function or constant
Wording
the coproduct congruence relation of M and N (from the name)
English
Let $M$ and $N$ be sets. Then the coproduct congruence relation of $M$ and $N$ is an additive congruence relation of the set of free additive monoids of $\mathrm{Sum}$ applied to $M$ and $N$ defined as the infimum of $\operatorname{ofPred}$ applied to the function that maps $c$ to the proposition that for all elements $x_{2}$ of $M$, for all elements $y$ of $M$, $c$ holds for $\operatorname{of}$ applied to $\operatorname{inl}$ applied to $x_{2} + y$ and $\operatorname{of} (\operatorname{inl} (x_{2}))+ \operatorname{of} (\operatorname{inl} (y))$, for all elements $x_{3}$ of $N$, for all elements $y_{1}$ of $N$, $c$ holds for $\operatorname{of}$ applied to $\operatorname{inr}$ applied to $x_{3} + y_{1}$ and $\operatorname{of} (\operatorname{inr} (x_{3}))+ \operatorname{of} (\operatorname{inr} (y_{1}))$, $c$ holds for $\operatorname{of}$ applied to $\operatorname{inl}$ applied to $0$ and $0$ and $c$ holds for $\operatorname{of}$ applied to $\operatorname{inr}$ applied to $0$ and $0$.

CoxeterMatrix.GroupdefMathlib.GroupTheory.Coxeter.Basic, type
Wording
group of M (from the name)
English
Let $M$ be a Coxeter matrix of $B$. Then a group of $M$ is an element of the function that maps $M$ to the set of presented groups of the relation set of $M$.
Formula
$M \in \mathrm{CoxeterMatrix} (B)\vdash \operatorname{Group} (M):= \lambda M . \mathrm{PresentedGroup} (\operatorname{relationsSet} (M))$
French
Soit $M$ une matrice de Coxeter de $B$. Alors un groupe de $M$ est un élément de la fonction qui envoie $M$ à l'ensemble de groupes présentés de l'ensemble de relations de $M$.
CoxeterMatrix.IsLiftabledefMathlib.GroupTheory.Coxeter.Basic, predicate
Wording
no wording
English
Let $M$ be a Coxeter matrix of $B$. Let $f$ be a function from elements of $B$ to elements of $G$. Then $\operatorname{IsLiftable}$ holds for $M$ and $f$, if for all elements $i$ and $\mathrm{i'}$ of $B$, $\EqtMACRoH {(f (i)\times f (\mathrm{i'}))^ {\operatorname{M} (M , i , \mathrm{i'})}}{1}$.
Formula
$M \in \mathrm{CoxeterMatrix} (B), f \in B \rightarrow G \vdash \operatorname{IsLiftable} (M , f):\Leftrightarrow \forall i \in B . \forall \mathrm{i'} \in B . \EqtMACRoJ {(f (i)\times f (\mathrm{i'}))^ {\operatorname{M} (M , i , \mathrm{i'})}}{1}$
French
Soit $M$ une matrice de Coxeter de $B$. Soit $f$ une fonction des éléments de $B$ à des éléments de $G$. Alors $\operatorname{IsLiftable}$ tient pour $M$ et $f$ si pour tous les éléments $i$ et $\mathrm{i'}$ de $B$, $(f (i)\times f (\mathrm{i'}))^ {\operatorname{M} (M , i , \mathrm{i'})}$ est égal à $1$.

CoxeterMatrix.relationdefMathlib.GroupTheory.Coxeter.Basic, function or constant
Wording
the relation of i and #3 (from the name)
English
Let $M$ be a Coxeter matrix of $B$. Let $i$ and $\mathrm{i'}$ be elements of $B$. Then the relation of $i$ and $\mathrm{i'}$ is a free group of $B$ defined as $(\operatorname{of} (i)\times \operatorname{of} (\mathrm{i'}))^ {\operatorname{M} (M , i , \mathrm{i'})}$.

Mathlib.GroupTheory.Coxeter.Inversion

CoxeterSystem.IsLeftInversiondefMathlib.GroupTheory.Coxeter.Inversion, predicate
Wording
no wording
English
Let $\mathrm{cs}$ be a Coxeter system of $M$ and $W$. Let $w$ and $t$ be elements of $W$. Then $\operatorname{IsLeftInversion}$ holds for $\mathrm{cs}$, $w$ and $t$, if $\operatorname{IsReflection}$ holds for $\mathrm{cs}$ and $t$ and the length of $\mathrm{cs}$ and $t \times w$ is less than the length of $\mathrm{cs}$ and $w$.
Formula
$\mathrm{cs} \in \mathrm{CoxeterSystem} (M , W), w , t \in W \vdash \operatorname{IsLeftInversion} (\mathrm{cs} , w , t):\Leftrightarrow \operatorname{IsReflection} (\mathrm{cs} , t)\wedge \operatorname{length} (\mathrm{cs} , t \times w)< \operatorname{length} (\mathrm{cs} , w)$
French
Soit $\mathrm{cs}$ un système de Coxeter de $M$ et $W$. Soient $w$ et $t$ des éléments de $W$. Alors $\operatorname{IsLeftInversion}$ tient pour $\mathrm{cs}$, $w$ et $t$ si $\operatorname{IsReflection}$ tient pour $\mathrm{cs}$ et $t$ et la longueur de $\mathrm{cs}$ et $t \times w$ est inférieure à la longueur de $\mathrm{cs}$ et $w$.

Formula
$\mathrm{cs} \in \mathrm{CoxeterSystem} (M , W), t \in W \vdash \operatorname{IsReflection} (\mathrm{cs} , t):\Leftrightarrow \exists w \in W . \exists i \in B . \EqtMACRoJ {t}{w \times \operatorname{simple} (\mathrm{cs} , i)\times \operatorname{inv} (w)}$
French

Alors $\operatorname{IsReflection}$ tient pour $\mathrm{cs}$ et $t$ s'il existe un élément $w$ de $W$ tel que il existe un élément $i$ de $B$ tel que $\EqtMACRoH {t}{w \times \operatorname{simple} (\mathrm{cs} , i)\times \operatorname{inv} (w)}$.

Formula
$e \in \mathrm{LinearEquiv} (\operatorname{id} (\Integer), \mathrm{FreeAbelianGroup} (x), \mathrm{FreeAbelianGroup} (x_{1}))\vdash \operatorname{ofFreeAbelianGroupLinearEquiv} (e):= \operatorname{indexEquiv} (\operatorname{map} (\operatorname{basis} (x), e), \operatorname{basis} (x_{1}))\in \EquivMACRoAL {x}{x_{1}}$
French
Soit $e$ une équivalence linéaire de l'ensemble de groupes abéliens libres de $x$ et l'ensemble de groupes abéliens libres de $x_{1}$. Alors l'équivalence de $e$ est une équivalence de $x$ à $x_{1}$ défini comme l'équivalence d'indices de l'application de la base de $x$ et $e$ et la base de $x_{1}$.

Let $G$ be a set. Then we can say that $G$ is free.
Formula
$G \in \mathrm{Set} \vdash \mathrm{IsFreeAddGroup} (G)\ \mathsf{prop}$
French
Soit $G$ un ensemble. Alors nous pouvons dire que $G$ est libre.

generator of G (from the name)
English
Let $G$ be a set. Then a generator of $G$ is an element of the function that maps $G$ to $\operatorname{choose}$ applied to $\mathrm{IsFreeAddGroup\_\_nonempty\_basis}$.
Formula
$G \in \mathrm{Set} \vdash \operatorname{Generators} (G):= \lambda G . \operatorname{choose} (\mathrm{IsFreeAddGroup\_\_nonempty\_basis})$

Soit $G$ un ensemble. Alors un générateur de $G$ est un élément de la fonction qui envoie $G$ à $\operatorname{choose}$ appliqué à $\mathrm{IsFreeAddGroup\_\_nonempty\_basis}$.

IsFreeAddGroup.basisdefMathlib.GroupTheory.FreeGroup.IsFreeGroup, function or constant
Wording
the basis of G (from the name)
English
Let $G$ be a set. Then the basis of $G$ is a free additive group basis of the set of generators of $G$ and $G$ defined as $\operatorname{ofRepr}$ applied to the inverse of the additive equivalence of $G$.
Formula
$G \in \mathrm{Set} \vdash \operatorname{basis} (G):= \operatorname{ofRepr} (\operatorname{symm} (\operatorname{addEquiv} (G)))\in \mathrm{FreeAddGroupBasis} (\operatorname{Generators} (G), G)$
French
Soit $G$ un ensemble. Alors la base de $G$ est une base de groupes additive libre de l'ensemble de générateurs de $G$ et $G$ défini comme $\operatorname{ofRepr}$ appliqué à l'inverse de l'équivalence additive de $G$.
IsFreeAddGroup.liftdefMathlib.GroupTheory.FreeGroup.IsFreeGroup, function or constant
Wording
the lift (from the name)
English
The lift is an equivalence from the set of functions from generators of $G$ to elements of $H$ to the set of additive monoid homomorphisms of $G$ and $H$ defined as the lift of the basis of $G$.
Formula
$\operatorname{lift} := \operatorname{lift} (\operatorname{basis} (G))\in \EquivMACRoAL {(\mathrm{Elem} (\operatorname{Generators} (G))\rightarrow H)}{\mathrm{AddMonoidHom} (G , H)}$
French
Le relèvement est une équivalence de l'ensemble de fonctions des générateurs de $G$ à des éléments de $H$ à l'ensemble d'homomorphismes de monoïdes additifs de $G$ et $H$ défini comme le relèvement de la base de $G$.
IsFreeAddGroup.ofdefMathlib.GroupTheory.FreeGroup.IsFreeGroup, function or constant
Wording
no wording
English
Let $x_{1}$ be a generator of $G$. Then $\operatorname{of}$ applied to $x_{1}$ is an element of $G$ defined as $\operatorname{comp}$ applied to $\operatorname{toFun}$ applied to $\operatorname{toEquiv}$ applied to the additive equivalence of $G$, $\operatorname{of}$ and $x_{1}$.
Formula
$x_{1} \in \operatorname{Generators} (G)\vdash \operatorname{of} (x_{1}):= \operatorname{comp} (\operatorname{toFun} (\operatorname{toEquiv} (\operatorname{addEquiv} (G))), \operatorname{of} , x_{1})\in G$
French
Soit $x_{1}$ un générateur de $G$. Alors $\operatorname{of}$ appliqué à $x_{1}$ est un élément de $G$ défini comme $\operatorname{comp}$ appliqué à $\operatorname{toFun}$ appliqué à $\operatorname{toEquiv}$ appliqué à l'équivalence additive de $G$, $\operatorname{of}$ et $x_{1}$.
IsFreeAddGroup.toFreeAddGroupdefMathlib.GroupTheory.FreeGroup.IsFreeGroup, function or constant
Wording
the free additive group of G (from the name)
English
Let $G$ be a set. Then the free additive group of $G$ is an additive equivalence of $G$ and the set of free additive groups of the set of generators of $G$ defined as the inverse of the additive equivalence of $G$.
Formula
$G \in \mathrm{Set} \vdash \operatorname{toFreeAddGroup} (G):= \operatorname{symm} (\operatorname{addEquiv} (G))\in \mathrm{AddEquiv} (G , \mathrm{FreeAddGroup} (\operatorname{Generators} (G)))$
French
Soit $G$ un ensemble. Alors le groupe additif libre de $G$ est une équivalence additive de $G$ et l'ensemble de groupes additifs libres de l'ensemble de générateurs de $G$ défini comme l'inverse de l'équivalence additive de $G$.
